Interpretation and limitations

This audit inspects public server-delivered HTML and observed responses. Network timing does not measure Core Web Vitals; JSON-LD checks do not establish rich-result eligibility; language and relevance require editorial review. A noindex directive can be intentional. Pending pages require a resumed scan, and failed requests require availability diagnosis before any SEO conclusion.
